| 1 | Who this applies to; LF23 | Spanish IVA applies to the peninsula and Balearic Islands. The Canary Islands, Ceuta and Melilla are outside its territory; IGIC/IPSI are separate taxes. Establish the taxable person's business activity, establishment, transactions and filing administration before preparing the return ([LIVA arts. 3–5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740#a3)). This Guide covers ordinary-regime sole traders and companies, including ordinary mixed taxable/exempt activities. VAT groups, modules, retailer surcharge, agriculture, cash-accounting taxpayers, OSS/IOSS, travel agents, used goods, investment gold, fuel-depot payments and other special regimes need their own computation. A customer receiving a cash-scheme invoice still needs its special deduction timing. The ordinary foral result-allocation method is supplied below. Use the separate [Canary Islands IGIC](es-canary-islands-igic.md) and [Ceuta/Melilla IPSI](es-ceuta-melilla-ipsi.md) Guides for their supplied ordinary methods and stated limits. The precise remaining territorial and other VAT reservations do not imply zero liabilities. | LIVA3 territory | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740#a3 | a) En la República Federal de Alemania, la Isla de Helgoland y el territorio de Büsingen; en el Reino de España, Ceuta y Melilla y en la República Italiana, Livigno, en cuanto territorios no comprendidos en la Unión Aduanera. | 2026-10-28 | same primary quotation actually read for matching rule: Original-language quotation reused from today’s matching ordinaryVAT evidence. | 29 | The base / Establish the transaction before calculating VAT; LF83 | Use the full consideration under arts. 78–79, including taxable ancillary charges and price-linked subsidies; identify discounts, genuine damages and disbursements separately. For a VAT-inclusive gross amount at a confirmed rate r, base is `gross/(1+r)` and VAT is the difference. Income-tax retention reduces the customer's settlement, not the invoice's VAT base; processor/platform fees and withholding are separately reconciled. | 71 | The base / Retained special-regime and small-transaction gateways; LF154 | The **two 10,000 tests are different**. Art. 73 combines qualifying intra-EU distance goods and cross-border B2C telecommunications/broadcasting/electronic services. Its small-supplier origin rule requires the statutory single-Member-State establishment conditions and the relevant dispatch origin; it is unavailable when distance goods are dispatched wholly or partly from another Member State. Check both the preceding and current calendar year, change to destination treatment from the transaction exceeding the limit, and do not split a transaction. A destination election binds for at least two calendar years. By contrast, art. 14 concerns acquisitions by agriculture-regime operators, exclusively non-deducting taxpayers and non-business legal persons: the preceding-year amount must **not have reached 10,000**, and current-year non-subjection lasts only until that amount is reached. Count the entire acquisition without splitting. New means of transport and excise goods are excluded from that exemption and from its limit; voluntary acquisition taxation binds for at least two years ([LIVA arts. 14/68/70/73](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740#a14)). | LIVA73 distance/TBEthreshold | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740#a73 | Uno. | 73 | The base / Retained special-regime and small-transaction gateways; LF158 | **2026 simplified-regime entry: published AEAT route.** The current art. 122 ordinary non-agricultural receipts and purchases limbs are **150,000**, while the agricultural/forestry/livestock receipts limb is **250,000**; the displayed LIVA DT 13 extension ends in 2024. | 107 | Deadlines and filing / Current ordinary 303 mapping; LF208 | For a general-rule B2B service received in the Spanish VAT territory from a supplier established outside the EU, declare its full taxable base and output VAT in12/13. If the input is an ordinary service or current good, put its unprorated base in28 and its legally deductible VAT in29; an actual art108 tangible investment good uses30/31. These are domestic-located acquisitions for this classification, not customs imports merely because the supplier is overseas. Customs import input remains32–35, and qualifying intra-EU input36–39. Apply private-use, blocked-input and prorrata limits to the input quota; do not reduce output13 by the prorrata. A US service of1,000 at21%, shared within a60% deduction-entitling business, gives12=1,000,13=210,28=1,000,29=126 and84 net VAT before other transactions/credits. A service recorded as an accounting intangible does not become an art108 tangible investment good solely through capitalisation. ([LIVA arts.69/84/92/108](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740#a69); [AEAT2026 instructions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/todas-gestiones/impuestos-tasas/iva/modelo-303-iva-autoliquidacion_/instrucciones-2026/instrucciones-02-12-2t-4t-2026.html)). | 108 | Penalties and interest; LF218 | Voluntary late tax-due filing without prior request attracts **1% plus 1% for each complete month late** through twelve months, then **15% plus the applicable interest after twelve months**, under art. 27. The conditional prompt-payment reduction is **25%** of that surcharge. Underpayment offences require classification: **50%**, **50–100%** or **100–150%** under art. 191, with art. 188 reductions only if their conditions hold. Executive-collection surcharges are **5/10/20%** by stage, not additional automatic filing penalties. A no-economic-loss late ordinary return has the art. 198 **200** starting amount, halved where its no-request conditions apply. Use the interest rate for each actual delay segment; [the core IRPF Guide](es-income-tax.md#penalties-and-interest) records the sourced 2026 rate and conditions. VAT-specific and SII infringements must be separately classified rather than assigned the underpayment rate ([LGT arts. 26–28, 188, 191, 198](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2003-23186#a27); [LIVA art. 170](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740#a170)). | LGT27 initialrecargo | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2003-23186#a27 | 2. | 131 | Rates, bands and thresholds; LF61 | **Nonmotor LPG and kerosene, including the early interval.** For ordinary taxable deliveries, imports and intra-Community acquisitions of nonmotor LPG under excise heading **1.8** or nonmotor kerosene under **1.12**, apply **10% at tax points from 22 March through 30 June 2026**, including 22 March–29 April. [RDL 10/2026 preamble II and DF 2.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-9286) expressly explain the change as clarifying the original interpretation without changing its content; the amendment removes that restrictive introduction and keeps the product list. Its formal DF 4 commencement is 30 April. This source chain and [AEAT's explicit 22 March–30 June application](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/manual-iva-2026/capitulo-01-novedades-destacar-2026/tipos-impositivos.html) supply the ordinary early-interval result; no separate express retroactivity clause or 21% early gap is asserted. | 181 | The base / Then calculate partial exemption, distinct from private use; LF101 | 1. Classify activities before pooling their input VAT. Ordinary activities are different when assigned different CNAE groups. Identify the principal activity by the greatest preceding-year turnover. Distinct activities whose art. 104 deduction percentages differ from the principal activity’s by **more than 50 percentage points** form a separate sector; exactly 50 points does not satisfy that test. Keep the principal activity, its ancillary activities and distinct activities within the50-point difference in the principal sector. An activity is ancillary only if it contributes to the parent activity and its preceding-year turnover does not exceed **15%** of that parent’s; use reasonable current-year forecasts for a new ancillary activity and reconcile the actual outcome. Apply each sector’s deduction regime separately. For inputs used jointly across ordinary sectors, apply art. 104’s rounded aggregate percentage using the relevant sectors’ included operations; do not give the whole invoice to the taxable sector. Do not pool the sectors’ other inputs without the required AEAT common-regime authorisation. Special-regime sectors retain their own rules. ([LIVA arts. 9.1.c/101/104](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740#a9)). | 191 | The base / Investment goods and credit ledger; LF120 | For art108, exclude an item whose acquisition value is **below3,005.06**; equality is not excluded by that monetary limb. Apply this to the individual qualifying asset, not a yearly basket. For an ordinary acquisition with recoverable VAT, use acquisition value **excluding that recoverable VAT**, including directly attributable costs required to bring the asset into working condition. Thus3,000+630 recoverable VAT remains below the threshold despite a3,630 invoice total. A qualifying machine costing2,900 plus150 directly attributable installation has acquisition value3,050 and crosses the monetary limb; unrelated repairs/replacement parts do not become investment goods merely because expensive. Check the tangible/work-instrument/>one-year definition and the other exclusions separately. ([LIVA art.108](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740#a108); [officialKA-01732](https://dfb.microsoftcrmportals.com/es-ES/Articulo/?Code=KA-01732); [DGT V0055-19](https://petete.tributos.hacienda.gob.es/consultas/?num_consulta=V0055-19); [PGCNRV2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-19884)). PGC NRV 2/12 includes legally irrecoverable input VAT in **initial book acquisition cost**; later definitive-prorrata and investment-good adjustments do not change that initial valuation. **Source-derived irrecoverable-VAT method:** binding V0055-19 invokes PGC acquisition value when applying article108; PGC NRV2.1/12 expressly defines its irrecoverable-tax component. For an ordinary purchased tangible asset governed by that acquisition-price valuation, calculate `initial acquisition value = complete qualifying initial acquisition cost excluding VAT + associated legally irrecoverable VAT`. Include all applicable NRV2 cost components, not only the invoice price. This application of the defined tax component is a combined-source interpretation; V0055-19 itself decides commissioning expenses and does not expressly adjudicate a decisive irrecoverable-VAT fact pattern. Establish recovery rights independently: legally recoverable VAT stays excluded even if not yet claimed or refunded. Do not assume a mixed-private-use recovery fraction from the investment-good status whose threshold is being tested. The monetary result is already fixed where known acquisition costs before any VAT reach3,005.06, or where even those costs plus **all** related VAT remain below3,005.06. Thus 3,100 before VAT passes that limb; 2,400 + 504 VAT = 2,904 fails it under either treatment. These bounds do not themselves establish recoverable VAT or override the asset definition and exclusions. For an otherwise qualifying fully business-used purchased asset costing3,000 plus630 VAT, full legal recovery gives value3,000; no recovery gives3,630;90% recovery gives567 deductible and63 irrecoverable VAT, hence value3,063. At99% recovery, irrecoverable6.30 gives3,006.30. Settled irrecoverable amounts5.05/5.06/5.07 give values3,005.05/3,005.06/3,005.07: only the first fails the monetary limb. These supplied legal entitlements are inputs, not a new prorrata or rounding method. Zero recovery still means no initial deduction. The actual KA01732 invoice shows supported and deductible VAT both630, so its VAT-excluded3,000 example concerns full recovery. Later lawful prorrata/investment-good corrections do not recost the initial valuation under NRV12; an original error, price change or disputed initial entitlement needs its separate correction route. Accounting capitalisation alone never overrides article108's definition/exclusions, and passing the threshold never creates a deduction. ([LIVA 108](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740#a108); [DGT V0055-19,section 2](https://petete.tributos.hacienda.gob.es/consultas/?num_consulta=V0055-19); [PGC NRV 2/12](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-19884).) | LIVA108 strictmonetaryexclusion | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740#a108 | 5.º Cualquier otro bien cuyo valor de adquisición sea inferior a quinientas mil pesetas. | 2026-10-28 | rule |

A qualifying machine costing2,900 plus150 directly attributable installation has acquisition value3,050 and crosses the monetary limb; unrelated repairs/replacement parts do not become investment goods merely because expensive. Check the tangible/work-instrument/>one-year definition and the other exclusions separately. ([LIVA art.108](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740#a108); [officialKA-01732](https://dfb.microsoftcrmportals.com/es-ES/Articulo/?Code=KA-01732); [DGT V0055-19](https://petete.tributos.hacienda.gob.es/consultas/?num_consulta=V0055-19); [PGCNRV2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-19884)). PGC NRV 2/12 includes legally irrecoverable input VAT in **initial book acquisition cost**; later definitive-prorrata and investment-good adjustments do not change that initial valuation. **Source-derived irrecoverable-VAT method:** binding V0055-19 invokes PGC acquisition value when applying article108; PGC NRV2.1/12 expressly defines its irrecoverable-tax component. For an ordinary purchased tangible asset governed by that acquisition-price valuation, calculate `initial acquisition value = complete qualifying initial acquisition cost excluding VAT + associated legally irrecoverable VAT`. Include all applicable NRV2 cost components, not only the invoice price. This application of the defined tax component is a combined-source interpretation; V0055-19 itself decides commissioning expenses and does not expressly adjudicate a decisive irrecoverable-VAT fact pattern. Establish recovery rights independently: legally recoverable VAT stays excluded even if not yet claimed or refunded. Do not assume a mixed-private-use recovery fraction from the investment-good status whose threshold is being tested. The monetary result is already fixed where known acquisition costs before any VAT reach3,005.06, or where even those costs plus **all** related VAT remain below3,005.06. Thus 3,100 before VAT passes that limb; 2,400 + 504 VAT = 2,904 fails it under either treatment. These bounds do not themselves establish recoverable VAT or override the asset definition and exclusions. For an otherwise qualifying fully business-used purchased asset costing3,000 plus630 VAT, full legal recovery gives value3,000; no recovery gives3,630;90% recovery gives567 deductible and63 irrecoverable VAT, hence value3,063. At99% recovery, irrecoverable6.30 gives3,006.30. Settled irrecoverable amounts5.05/5.06/5.07 give values3,005.05/3,005.06/3,005.07: only the first fails the monetary limb. These supplied legal entitlements are inputs, not a new prorrata or rounding method. Zero recovery still means no initial deduction. The actual KA01732 invoice shows supported and deductible VAT both630, so its VAT-excluded3,000 example concerns full recovery. Later lawful prorrata/investment-good corrections do not recost the initial valuation under NRV12; an original error, price change or disputed initial entitlement needs its separate correction route. Accounting capitalisation alone never overrides article108's definition/exclusions, and passing the threshold never creates a deduction. The text does not instruct freezing the factor whose gate did not open. Keep the actual filing proof; continued limits do not reopen the window or establish a particular return correction. | 241 | Deadlines and filing / Correcting an earlier ordinary303; LF212 | For a2026 Modelo303, correct the same period using an autoliquidación rectificativa, retaining all unchanged data as well as corrected/new data and identifying the earlier return. This covers an increased amount payable and a reduced amount payable/refund/credit, subject to prescription and the rules for already assessed elements. An earlier-return rectification concerning VAT improperly charged to another taxpayer uses the RGAT129 request procedure, outside the ordinary rectificativa route; the supplier can alternatively regularise under [LIVA89.Cinco(b)](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740#a89), subject to its one-year limit and reimbursement obligation. OSS-regime corrections follow their own regime. If the sole ground is a reasoned claim that the applied rule breaches a superior legal, constitutional, EU or treaty norm, choose the art120.3 rectification-request procedure or a rectificativa; when other grounds also exist, those other grounds require a rectificativa. A disagreement with an administrative interpretation is a separate form reason: box108 is exceptional and can be used only with that reason where the adjustment cannot be entered in the normal boxes. Do not confuse correcting an earlier return with a later art80 transaction/base change whose own adjustment period is prescribed by law. | RIVA74bis ordinarycorrection | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28925#a7-2 | 1. If the sole ground is a reasoned claim that the applied rule breaches a superior legal, constitutional, EU or treaty norm, choose the art120.3 rectification-request procedure or a rectificativa; when other grounds also exist, those other grounds require a rectificativa. A disagreement with an administrative interpretation is a separate form reason: box108 is exceptional and can be used only with that reason where the adjustment cannot be entered in the normal boxes. Do not confuse correcting an earlier return with a later art80 transaction/base change whose own adjustment period is prescribed by law. | LIVA89.5.b alternative/reimbursement | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740#a89 | b) Regularizar la situación tributaria en la declaración-liquidación correspondiente al periodo en que deba efectuarse la rectificación o en las posteriores hasta el plazo de un año a contar desde el momento en que debió efectuarse la mencionada rectificación. En este caso, el sujeto pasivo estará obligado a reintegrar al destinatario de la operación el importe de las cuotas repercutidas en exceso. | 2026-10-28 | rule |

| 245 | Penalties and interest; LF218 | Voluntary late tax-due filing without prior request attracts **1% plus 1% for each complete month late** through twelve months, then **15% plus the applicable interest after twelve months**, under art. 27. General recovery would be **6,000**; special recovery is **1,000 + 3,000 × 60% = 2,800**. Since **6,000 ≥ 1.10 × 2,800 = 3,080**, special is compulsory. | 182 | **Minimum viable.** Bank statement for the period in CSV, PDF, or pasted text. It must cover the full period. Any Spanish or international business bank is acceptable: CaixaBank, Santander, BBVA, Bankinter, Sabadell, ING Direct, Revolut Business, Wise Business, N26 Business, or another. | Current LF25 and LF288–293 obtain actual registers, invoices, census, use, credit and elections. Bank-only source and materiality workflow preserved in historical body; no unsupported legal deduction from a bank line. |
| 184 | **Recommended.** Sales invoices for the period (above all for intra-EU business-to-business services and exempt supplies), and purchase invoices (facturas) for input VAT claims. A simplified invoice (factura simplificada) may be issued up to the amount in the invoice table below, but it only supports a deduction if it identifies the recipient and shows the VAT quota separately. Get the client's NIF-IVA in writing. | Current LF25 and LF288–293 obtain actual registers, invoices, census, use, credit and elections. Bank-only source and materiality workflow preserved in historical body; no unsupported legal deduction from a bank line. |

| 198 | **Refusal policy if the minimum is missing: soft warn.** If no bank statement is available at all, hard stop. If there is a bank statement but no invoices, proceed but record in the reviewer brief: "This modelo 303 was produced from a bank statement alone. The reviewer must verify, before approval, that input VAT claims are supported by compliant facturas, including recipient identification and a separate VAT quota on any factura simplificada, and that every reverse-charge classification matches the supplier's invoice." | Current LF23/87/101–162/167–173/222–226 replaces blanket IGIC/IPSI/prorrata/special-regime/foral refusal with positive named ordinary methods plus exact remaining specialist boundaries. VAT groups and full margin/module computations remain outside supplied ordinary general-return method. |
| 202 | These refusals apply on top of the EU-wide refusals in `eu-vat-directive` Section 13 (R-EU-1 through R-EU-12). If any trigger fires, stop, output the refusal message verbatim, end the conversation. | Current LF23/87/101–162/167–173/222–226 replaces blanket IGIC/IPSI/prorrata/special-regime/foral refusal with positive named ordinary methods plus exact remaining specialist boundaries. VAT groups and full margin/module computations remain outside supplied ordinary general-return method. |
| 204 | - **R-ES-1, Canary Islands (IGIC).** Trigger: the client is in the Canary Islands or asks about IGIC. Message: "The Canary Islands use IGIC (Impuesto General Indirecto Canario), not IVA. This Guide covers IVA only, that is peninsular Spain and the Balearic Islands. Please use an Asesor Fiscal familiar with IGIC." | Current LF23/87/101–162/167–173/222–226 replaces blanket IGIC/IPSI/prorrata/special-regime/foral refusal with positive named ordinary methods plus exact remaining specialist boundaries. VAT groups and full margin/module computations remain outside supplied ordinary general-return method. |
| 205 | - **R-ES-2, Ceuta or Melilla (IPSI).** Trigger: the client is in Ceuta or Melilla or asks about IPSI. Message: "Ceuta and Melilla use IPSI (Impuesto sobre la Producción, los Servicios y la Importación), not IVA. Out of scope for this Guide." | Current LF23/87/101–162/167–173/222–226 replaces blanket IGIC/IPSI/prorrata/special-regime/foral refusal with positive named ordinary methods plus exact remaining specialist boundaries. VAT groups and full margin/module computations remain outside supplied ordinary general-return method. |
| 206 | - **R-ES-3, régimen simplificado (modules).** Trigger: the client files under the régimen simplificado, boxes 47 to 58 of the form. Message: "The régimen simplificado computes VAT from activity modules, not from transaction classification. This Guide covers the régimen general only. Please use an Asesor Fiscal for module-based returns." | Current LF23/87/101–162/167–173/222–226 replaces blanket IGIC/IPSI/prorrata/special-regime/foral refusal with positive named ordinary methods plus exact remaining specialist boundaries. VAT groups and full margin/module computations remain outside supplied ordinary general-return method. |
| 207 | - **R-ES-4, recargo de equivalencia.** Trigger: the client is a retailer inside the recargo de equivalencia scheme. Message: "Recargo de equivalencia is a scheme for retailers who are individuals or entities in the IRPF attribution regime and who sell goods without transforming them (art. 148 and 149 LIVA). Their suppliers charge the surcharge in the rate table on top of VAT, and the retailer does not self-assess or deduct VAT on those retail sales (art. 154.Dos LIVA). Note that the retailer must still self-assess intra-EU acquisitions and operations with reverse charge under art. 84.Uno.2 LIVA (art. 154.Uno LIVA). This Guide covers the régimen general only." | Current LF23/87/101–162/167–173/222–226 replaces blanket IGIC/IPSI/prorrata/special-regime/foral refusal with positive named ordinary methods plus exact remaining specialist boundaries. VAT groups and full margin/module computations remain outside supplied ordinary general-return method. |
| 208 | - **R-ES-5, RECC cash-basis scheme.** Trigger: the client uses the régimen especial del criterio de caja. Message: "The RECC scheme moves the tax point to the payment date for both output and input VAT, with 31 December of the following year as the backstop, and it has its own boxes 62, 63, 74 and 75. If your client is not in the scheme but buys from a supplier who is, do not refuse: boxes 74 and 75 still have to be completed for those purchases. This Guide assumes the general accrual tax point (devengo). Please use an Asesor Fiscal for RECC returns." | Current LF23/87/101–162/167–173/222–226 replaces blanket IGIC/IPSI/prorrata/special-regime/foral refusal with positive named ordinary methods plus exact remaining specialist boundaries. VAT groups and full margin/module computations remain outside supplied ordinary general-return method. |
| 209 | - **R-ES-6, prorrata (partial exemption).** Trigger: the client makes both taxable and exempt supplies (art. 20 LIVA) and the exempt part is not trivial. Message: "Your business makes both taxable and exempt supplies. Input VAT must be apportioned under the prorrata rules (art. 102 to 106 LIVA), and the definitive annual percentage is settled in box 44 of the last return of the year. Please use an Asesor Fiscal to determine the prorrata percentage before input VAT is claimed." | Current LF23/87/101–162/167–173/222–226 replaces blanket IGIC/IPSI/prorrata/special-regime/foral refusal with positive named ordinary methods plus exact remaining specialist boundaries. VAT groups and full margin/module computations remain outside supplied ordinary general-return method. |
| 210 | - **R-ES-7, VAT groups (grupos de entidades).** Trigger: the client is in a VAT group under art. 163 quinquies to nonies LIVA. Message: "VAT group consolidation is out of scope, and a group files modelo 322 and modelo 353, not this form. Please use an Asesor Fiscal." | Current LF23/87/101–162/167–173/222–226 replaces blanket IGIC/IPSI/prorrata/special-regime/foral refusal with positive named ordinary methods plus exact remaining specialist boundaries. VAT groups and full margin/module computations remain outside supplied ordinary general-return method. |
| 211 | - **R-ES-8, margin schemes.** Trigger: the client deals in second-hand goods, travel agency packages, or art and antiques under a margin scheme. Message: "Margin scheme regimes (bienes usados, agencias de viajes, objetos de arte) need a transaction-level margin computation. Out of scope." | Current LF23/87/101–162/167–173/222–226 replaces blanket IGIC/IPSI/prorrata/special-regime/foral refusal with positive named ordinary methods plus exact remaining specialist boundaries. VAT groups and full margin/module computations remain outside supplied ordinary general-return method. |

| 430 | Gross 2,999 euros, net 2,478.51 euros. A good is an investment good when it is normally meant to be used for more than a year as an instrument of work, and it is not one when its value is below the limit in Section 5.10. Both readings here are below that limit, so the purchase goes to boxes 28 and 29, not to boxes 30 and 31. If accessories on the same invoice push the value over, reclassify. The official page says "valor" without saying whether VAT is included, so where the two readings differ, ask the reviewer. | Current LF49/120/122 and LF196: strict 3005.06 acquisition-value boundary plus asset definition; old 2999/2478.51/520.49 example preserved here as computed historical illustration, not statutory amount. Installation/incorporated legally irrecoverable VAT now expressly handled as labelled combined-source method. |

| 468 | - **Standard rate default.** The default for any taxable supply unless a reduced rate, an exemption or a reverse charge applies. Sales go to boxes 07, 08 and 09; purchases to boxes 28 and 29. _(Art. 90 LIVA)_ | Current LF29/33–35/193/196 actual product-based statutory rates; dated exceptions at LF37–77 supersede ordinary-only historic assumption. |
| 472 | - **Reduced rate.** Art. 91.Uno LIVA lists it: food and drink outside the super-reduced list, water, passenger transport, hotels, restaurant and catering, the first transfer of new housing, qualifying home renovation, certain medical equipment. Sales go to boxes 04, 05 and 06; purchases to boxes 28 and 29, which are not split by rate. _(Art. 91.Uno LIVA)_ | Current LF29/33–35/193/196 actual product-based statutory rates; dated exceptions at LF37–77 supersede ordinary-only historic assumption. |
| 476 | - **Super-reduced rate.** Art. 91.Dos LIVA lists it: plain bread and bread dough, bread flour, milk, cheese, eggs, natural fruit, vegetables, pulses, tubers and cereals, olive oil, books, newspapers and magazines, medicines for human use, certain disability aids and social housing. Olive oil has been in this list since 1 January 2025. Sales go to boxes 01, 02 and 03; purchases to boxes 28 and 29. _(Art. 91.Dos LIVA)_ | Current LF29/33–35/193/196 actual product-based statutory rates; dated exceptions at LF37–77 supersede ordinary-only historic assumption. |
| 480 | - **Exports and intra-EU supplies.** Exports outside the Union are exempt with a right of deduction (art. 21 LIVA) and go in box 60, together with sendings to the Canary Islands, Ceuta and Melilla. Intra-EU supplies of goods (art. 25 LIVA) and intra-EU services to a business customer (art. 69 LIVA) go in box 59 and on modelo 349. The form's zero-rate row (boxes 150, 151 and 152) is a different thing from these exemptions. _(Art. 21, 25 and 69 LIVA)_ | Current LF29/33–35/193/196 actual product-based statutory rates; dated exceptions at LF37–77 supersede ordinary-only historic assumption. |
| 484 | - **Exempt without credit.** Medical services, education, insurance, financial services, the universal postal service, residential letting, social welfare. No output VAT and no deduction of the input VAT behind them. If they are more than trivial, R-ES-6 fires. _(Art. 20 LIVA)_ | Current LF87/224/226/202 and LF91: exact exemptions, preservation of input rights and reporting. Old triviality trigger is not a statutory de minimis exception to prorrata; LF101–107 supplies proper method. |
| 488 | - **Local purchases.** Input VAT on a compliant factura from a Spanish supplier is deductible where the purchase is used in the taxable activity, subject to 5.12 and to the investment-goods split in 5.10. Boxes 28 and 29, or 30 and 31. Only the holder of the invoice may deduct (art. 97 LIVA), and the deduction must be taken within four years of the right arising (art. 99.Tres LIVA). | Current LF87/224/226/202 and LF91: exact exemptions, preservation of input rights and reporting. Old triviality trigger is not a statutory de minimis exception to prorrata; LF101–107 supplies proper method. |
| 492 | - **EU services.** A service from a supplier established in another member state, invoiced without VAT and with a reverse-charge note, is an intra-EU acquisition of services: output in boxes 10 and 11, input in boxes 36 and 37, net effect zero. The form puts intra-EU goods and services in the same pair. If the supplier charged its own national VAT, that is not a reverse charge: it is foreign VAT, not deductible here, and only the cross-border refund procedure recovers it. _(Art. 84.Uno.2 LIVA)_ | Current LF83/91/194–198/208/224/226: documentary entitlement, actual place/reverse-charge/import route and row selection. General foreign VAT cannot be deducted as Spanish VAT; elected import deferral is retained LF226. |
| 496 | - **EU goods.** An intra-EU acquisition of goods: output in boxes 10 and 11, input in boxes 36 and 37, or 38 and 39 for investment goods. _(Art. 13 LIVA)_ | Current LF83/91/194–198/208/224/226: documentary entitlement, actual place/reverse-charge/import route and row selection. General foreign VAT cannot be deducted as Spanish VAT; elected import deferral is retained LF226. |
| 500 | - **Non-EU services and imports.** A service from a supplier outside the Union is still a reverse charge under art. 84.Uno.2 LIVA but not an intra-EU acquisition: output in boxes 12 and 13, input in boxes 28 and 29 as an operación interior. Imported goods are different: import VAT is assessed by customs on the DUA and deducted in boxes 32 and 33, or 34 and 35 for investment goods. A monthly filer who elected the deferral by census declaration in the November before the year may declare the customs-assessed import VAT in box 77 instead of paying it at the border (art. 74.1 RIVA). Without that election box 77 stays empty. _(Art. 84.Uno.2 LIVA)_ | Current LF83/91/194–198/208/224/226: documentary entitlement, actual place/reverse-charge/import route and row selection. General foreign VAT cannot be deducted as Spanish VAT; elected import deferral is retained LF226. |
| 504 | - **Bienes de inversión.** A tangible good normally meant to be used for more than a year as an instrument of work (art. 108.Uno LIVA). Art. 108.Dos excludes spare parts, repair work, packaging, work clothing, and any good whose acquisition value is below the amount below. They go in boxes 30 and 31 (domestic), 34 and 35 (imports) or 38 and 39 (intra-EU). The deduction is regularised over the four calendar years after acquisition, or the nine years after it for land and buildings, and only where a year's definitive percentage differs from the original by more than ten points (art. 107 LIVA). | Current LF83/91/194–198/208/224/226: documentary entitlement, actual place/reverse-charge/import route and row selection. General foreign VAT cannot be deducted as Spanish VAT; elected import deferral is retained LF226. |

| 529 | - **Excluded categories.** Art. 96.Uno LIVA excludes from deduction in any proportion, with their accessories: jewellery and objects of gold or platinum (96.Uno.1); food, drink and tobacco (96.Uno.3); shows and recreational services (96.Uno.4); goods and services meant as hospitality for clients, employees or third parties (96.Uno.5); and travel, hotel and restaurant services unless the amount is deductible for income tax or corporate tax (96.Uno.6). Number 2 of the list was repealed. Art. 96.Dos puts back goods of exclusively industrial, commercial, agricultural, clinical or scientific use, and goods and services bought for resale by a business that does that for a living. Free samples and low-value advertising objects are not hospitality, but art. 7.4 LIVA stops treating an advertising object as low-value once the total to one recipient in a calendar year passes the amount below. | Current LF48/93/95/136–144 and LF232: rebuttable vehicle use, other partly private assets/utilities, actual later-use proof and combined separate-gate adjustment; no universal presumed recovery for unproved non-vehicle input. |

| 540 | - **SII.** Anyone whose period is the calendar month must keep the VAT registers through the Sede electrónica by supplying the invoice records electronically (art. 62.6 RIVA); anyone else may opt in (art. 68 bis RIVA). The supply deadlines are in the deadline table in Section 1. Where the client is inside the SII, note in the reviewer brief that the return must agree with the records supplied, because the Agencia Tributaria builds its draft from them. | Current LF42/97/150: statutory restricted inputs/exceptions and advertising intrinsic-value/indelible mark/200 annual recipient test, not a blanket all-small-gifts allowance. |
| 544 | - **Modelo 390.** An annual informative return summarising the year's modelo 303 filings; its deadline is in the deadline table in Section 1. The 2026 instructions exonerate two groups: quarterly filers taxed only in territorio común whose only activities are the simplified regime or letting urban property, and anyone keeping the registers through the Sede electrónica. Both instead complete the extra data block on page 4 of the last modelo 303 of the year. Neither exoneration applies where there is no duty to file that last return. | Current LF42/97/150: statutory restricted inputs/exceptions and advertising intrinsic-value/indelible mark/200 annual recipient test, not a blanket all-small-gifts allowance. |
| 548 | - **Modelo 349.** Declares intra-EU supplies of goods, intra-EU services supplied and intra-EU acquisitions. Monthly as a general rule, quarterly only under the test in the deadline table in Section 1. It must agree with boxes 10, 11 and 59 of the modelo 303 filings for the same period. | Current LF42/97/150: statutory restricted inputs/exceptions and advertising intrinsic-value/indelible mark/200 annual recipient test, not a blanket all-small-gifts allowance. |
| 552 | For each ambiguity: the pattern, why the bank statement is not enough, the conservative default, and the question to ask. | Current LF42/97/150: statutory restricted inputs/exceptions and advertising intrinsic-value/indelible mark/200 annual recipient test, not a blanket all-small-gifts allowance. |

| 792 | 1. **Fix the period and the regime.** Quarter or month is decided by art. 71.3 RIVA, and the monthly cases are listed in the deadline table in Section 1 ([RIVA](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28925)). A monthly period also means the VAT registers must be kept through the Sede electrónica (art. 62.6 RIVA). Check whether any refusal in Section 2 fires before doing anything else. | Current LF23/25/154–173/179–208/288–293 establishes actual entity/territory/regime/census/elections, period, exempt turnover, credit and counterparties. LF158–162 supplies actual published2026 simplified eligibility/window; historic150k baseline is not silently used as2026 practical higher-limit route. |
| 793 | 2. **Classify every transaction against Ley 37/1992.** Rate, exemption, place of supply and reverse charge all come from the law, not from the bank narrative ([Ley 37/1992](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740)). Use the supplier library in Section 3 first, the Tier 1 rules in Section 5 next, and the Tier 2 defaults in Section 6 only where the data cannot answer the question. | Current LF23/25/154–173/179–208/288–293 establishes actual entity/territory/regime/census/elections, period, exempt turnover, credit and counterparties. LF158–162 supplies actual published2026 simplified eligibility/window; historic150k baseline is not silently used as2026 practical higher-limit route. |
| 794 | 3. **Fill the output side of the modelo 303.** Put each rate band in its own row of boxes, intra-EU acquisitions of goods and services in boxes 10 and 11, every other reverse charge in boxes 12 and 13, and base or quota changes in boxes 14 and 15. Box 27 is the total ([the form, Orden HAC/27/2026](https://www.boe.es/boe/dias/2026/01/26/pdfs/BOE-A-2026-1761.pdf)). | Current LF23/25/154–173/179–208/288–293 establishes actual entity/territory/regime/census/elections, period, exempt turnover, credit and counterparties. LF158–162 supplies actual published2026 simplified eligibility/window; historic150k baseline is not silently used as2026 practical higher-limit route. |
| 795 | 4. **Fill the input side.** Split the deductible quotas between operaciones interiores, imports and intra-EU acquisitions, and within each between corrientes and bienes de inversión, then add the rectifications and regularisations. Box 45 is the total and box 46 is the difference ([the form, Orden HAC/27/2026](https://www.boe.es/boe/dias/2026/01/26/pdfs/BOE-A-2026-1761.pdf)). | Current LF23/25/154–173/179–208/288–293 establishes actual entity/territory/regime/census/elections, period, exempt turnover, credit and counterparties. **Not yet confirmed:** the exact legal reconciliation beyond AEAT's published post-repeal explanation, or case-specific correction of a filed election/return; the published AEAT entry/election route is supplied above. The ordinary published AEAT exactly-10-kW route and the literal-source reconciliation are supplied above. Also unresolved: December's October CPI outcome is not yet available at this read date; the fact-specific authorised common deduction-regime election beyond the supplied ordinary sector and startup computations; authoritative reconciliation of the supplied literal DGT post-adjustment reference method with the contrary acquisition-reference wording/calculator for delayed first use with annual P movement only; the specialised fuel box-112 result conflict; special foral allocation facts beyond the ordinary method above. Canary IGIC and Ceuta/Melilla IPSI use their dedicated Guides linked below. These are retained coverage limits, not assumed zero credits or blanket refusal of ordinary partial exemption.
